Detect via timing attacks, differential responses, and tools like Burp's HTTP Request Smuggler extension. Use this to bypass security controls, hijack user sessions, and poison web caches. ([ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge](https://github.com/ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge/tree/HEAD/skills/curated/http-request-smuggling-desync) · ★22)
- **http-request-smuggling-te-te**: Exploit advanced HTTP Request Smuggling combining Transfer-Encoding vulnerabilities (TE.TE). ([ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge](https://github.com/ShulkwiSEC/bb-huge/tree/HEAD/skills/curated/http-request-smuggling-te-te) · ★22)
